ID: fa06e64a03d9 | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n)

Mutation test: General theorem - no specific hypothesis removable

Extra proofs (3 theorems from expanded bank)

Proof 1: Riemann mapping theorem: simply connected domain not equal to C maps conformally to unit disk

Statement: Riemann mapping theorem: simply connected domain not equal to C maps conformally to unit disk

Hypotheses: U simply connected, U != C

Conclusion: exists biholomorphism f: U -> D

Definitions: Riemann mapping, conformal, simply connected

Proof:

Step 1: Consider family of injective holomorphic f: U -> D

Step 2: Show family nonempty by explicit construction

Step 3: Montel theorem: normal family

Step 4: Maximize |f'(z_0)|, show maximizer is surjective

Step 5: Surjective + injective => biholomorphism

ID: 9c5bd20099b9 | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n)

Mutation test: Without 'simply connected': may have nontrivial topology

Provenance: Domain Complex, theorem #1

Proof 2: Argument principle: (1/2pi i) integral f'/f = Z - P inside contour

Statement: Argument principle: (1/2pi i) integral f'/f = Z - P inside contour

Hypotheses: f meromorphic inside gamma, gamma simple closed

Conclusion: integral gamma f'/f dz = 2pi i (Z-P)

Definitions: argument principle, zeros, poles

Proof:

Step 1: Near zero of order m: f'/f ~ m/(z-z_0)

Step 2: Near pole of order n: f'/f ~ -n/(z-z_0)

Step 3: Residue of f'/f at zero is m

Step 4: Residue of f'/f at pole is -n

Step 5: Sum residues: Z - P

ID: 48459bf469d4 | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n^2)

Mutation test: Counting zeros and poles via contour integral

Provenance: Domain Complex, theorem #2

Proof 3: Liouville theorem: bounded entire function is constant

Statement: Liouville theorem: bounded entire function is constant

Hypotheses: f: C -> C entire, |f(z)| <= M

Conclusion: f is constant

Definitions: Liouville, bounded, entire

Proof:

Step 1: Cauchy estimate: |f'(z)| <= M/R for all R

Step 2: Let R -> infinity: f'(z) = 0

Step 3: f constant by connectedness of C

Statement: Several complex variables: Hartogs extension: holomorphic extends across codim >= 2
